63: // ADR-0016 Decision B (2026-09-25): the avulso form's own per-call override of A22's tenant-wide
64: // delivery default - "Usar padrão da organização" (DEFAULT) resolves against
65: // `RequestDeliverySettings.tsx`'s configured preference, never a fabricated third state. Series
66: // have no equivalent control (the ADR is explicit: A22 governs recorrentes with no per-series
67: // override).
68: const DELIVERY_OVERRIDE_OPTIONS: { value: InitialInviteDeliveryOverride; label: string; hint: string }[] = [
69:   { value: "DEFAULT", label: "Usar padrão da organização", hint: "Segue a configuração em Configurações > Entrega de solicitação." },
70:   { value: "MANUAL", label: "Entrega manual", hint: "Nenhum e-mail automático é enviado para esta solicitação." },
71:   { value: "EMAIL", label: "E-mail automático", hint: "O link é enviado por e-mail no momento da criação, exige um destinatário." },
72: ];
73: 
74: const WRITE_ROLES: ReadonlySet<MembershipRole> = new Set(["OWNER", "ADMIN", "MEMBER"]);
75: 
76: const NAMED_CADENCES: { value: string; label: string; intervalDays: number }[] = [
77:   { value: "MONTHLY", label: "Mensal", intervalDays: 30 },
78:   { value: "QUARTERLY", label: "Trimestral", intervalDays: 90 },
79:   { value: "SEMIANNUAL", label: "Semestral", intervalDays: 180 },
80:   { value: "ANNUAL", label: "Anual", intervalDays: 365 },
81: ];
82: 
83: function cadenceLabel(intervalDays: number): string {
84:   const named = NAMED_CADENCES.find((c) => c.intervalDays === intervalDays);
85:   return named ? named.label : `A cada ${intervalDays} dias`;
86: }
87: 
88: function isPendingAvulso(request: DocumentRequest): boolean {
89:   return !request.seriesId && (request.status === "REQUESTED" || request.status === "OPENED");
90: }
91: 
92: export function SubjectRequests() {
93:   const { subjectId = "", seriesId: seriesIdParam } = useParams<{ subjectId: string; seriesId?: string }>();
94:   const orgPath = useOrgPath();
95:   const navigate = useNavigate();
96:   const role = useCurrentMembershipRole();
97:   const canWrite = role !== undefined && WRITE_ROLES.has(role);
98:   const { showToast } = useToast();
99: 
100:   const subjectQuery = useSubject(subjectId);
101:   const requirementsQuery = useRequirementsForSubject(subjectId);
102:   const seriesQuery = useDocumentRequestSeries(subjectId);
103:   const requestsQuery = useDocumentRequestsForSubject(subjectId);
104: 
105:   const [showAvulsoDialog, setShowAvulsoDialog] = useState(false);
106:   const [seriesDialog, setSeriesDialog] = useState<{ mode: "create" } | { mode: "edit"; series: DocumentRequestSeries } | undefined>();
107:   const [cancelingSeries, setCancelingSeries] = useState<DocumentRequestSeries | undefined>();
108:   const [viewingRequest, setViewingRequest] = useState<DocumentRequest | undefined>();
109: 
110:   if (subjectQuery.isPending) return <InitialLoading label="Carregando fornecedor…" />;
111:   if (subjectQuery.isError) {
112:     const message = subjectQuery.error instanceof ApiError ? subjectQuery.error.message : "Não foi possível carregar este fornecedor.";
113:     return <ErrorState message={message} onRetry={() => void subjectQuery.refetch()} />;
114:   }
115:   const subject = subjectQuery.data.subject;
116: 
117:   const requirements = requirementsQuery.data?.requirements ?? [];
118:   const series = seriesQuery.data?.series ?? [];
119:   const requests = requestsQuery.data?.documentRequests ?? [];
120: 
121:   const requirementsWithoutPendingAvulso = requirements.filter((r) => !requests.some((req) => req.requirementId === r.requirementId && isPendingAvulso(req)));
122:   const requirementsWithoutActiveSeries = requirements.filter((r) => !series.some((s) => s.requirementId === r.requirementId && s.status === "ACTIVE"));
123: 
124:   const activeSeriesFromParam = seriesIdParam ? series.find((s) => s.seriesId === seriesIdParam) : undefined;
125: 
126:   function requirementName(requirementId: string): string {
127:     return requirements.find((r) => r.requirementId === requirementId)?.name ?? requirementId;
128:   }
129: 
130:   return (
131:     <div>
132:       <PageHeader
133:         above={<Link to={orgPath(`/subjects/${subjectId}`)}>← Voltar para {subject.displayName}</Link>}
134:         title="Solicitações e recorrência"
135:         description={`${subject.displayName} · geração de solicitações de documento a partir de Requisitos.`}
136:         actions={
137:           canWrite ? (
138:             <>
139:               <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setShowAvulsoDialog(true)}>
140:                 Nova solicitação avulsa
141:               </Button>{" "}
142:               <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setSeriesDialog({ mode: "create" })}>
143:                 Nova série recorrente
144:               </Button>
145:             </>
146:           ) : undefined
147:         }
148:       />
149: 
150:       <Section heading="Séries recorrentes" headingId="series-heading" annotation={`(${series.length})`}>
151:         {seriesQuery.isPending ? (
152:           <CollectionSkeleton rows={3} label="Carregando séries…" />
153:         ) : seriesQuery.isError ? (
154:           <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => void seriesQuery.refetch()}>Tentar novamente</Button>}>
155:             Não foi possível carregar as séries recorrentes.
156:           </InlineNotice>
157:         ) : series.length === 0 ? (
158:           <EmptyState
159:             kind="true-empty"
160:             message="Nenhuma série recorrente configurada. Crie uma série para receber este documento periodicamente sem ação manual."
161:             action={canWrite ? <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setSeriesDialog({ mode: "create" })}>Nova série recorrente</Button> : undefined}
162:           />
163:         ) : (
164:           <Panel>
165:             <SeriesTable
166:               series={series}
167:               requirementName={requirementName}
168:               canWrite={canWrite}
169:               orgPath={orgPath}
170:               subjectId={subjectId}
171:               onEdit={(s) => setSeriesDialog({ mode: "edit", series: s })}
172:               onCancel={(s) => setCancelingSeries(s)}
173:               showToast={showToast}
174:             />
175:           </Panel>
176:         )}
177:       </Section>
178: 
179:       <Section heading="Solicitações avulsas e materializações" headingId="requests-heading" annotation={`(${requests.length})`}>
180:         {requestsQuery.isPending ? (
181:           <CollectionSkeleton rows={3} label="Carregando solicitações…" />
182:         ) : requestsQuery.isError ? (
183:           <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => void requestsQuery.refetch()}>Tentar novamente</Button>}>
184:             Não foi possível carregar as solicitações.
185:           </InlineNotice>
186:         ) : requests.length === 0 ? (
187:           <EmptyState
188:             kind="true-empty"
189:             message="Nenhuma solicitação avulsa registrada. Crie uma solicitação avulsa para pedir este documento uma única vez."
190:             action={canWrite ? <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setShowAvulsoDialog(true)}>Nova solicitação avulsa</Button> : undefined}
191:           />
192:         ) : (
193:           <Panel>
194:             <RequestsTable requests={requests} requirementName={requirementName} onView={(r) => setViewingRequest(r)} />
195:           </Panel>
196:         )}
197:       </Section>
198: 
199:       {showAvulsoDialog ? (
200:         <CreateAvulsoDialog subjectId={subjectId} requirements={requirementsWithoutPendingAvulso} onClose={() => setShowAvulsoDialog(false)} showToast={showToast} />
201:       ) : null}
202: 
203:       {seriesDialog ? (
204:         <SeriesDialog
205:           subjectId={subjectId}
206:           mode={seriesDialog}
207:           requirements={requirementsWithoutActiveSeries}
208:           onClose={() => setSeriesDialog(undefined)}
209:           showToast={showToast}
210:         />
211:       ) : null}
212: 
213:       {cancelingSeries ? (
214:         <CancelSeriesDialog subjectId={subjectId} series={cancelingSeries} requirementName={requirementName} onClose={() => setCancelingSeries(undefined)} showToast={showToast} />
215:       ) : null}
216: 
217:       {viewingRequest ? <RequestDetailDialog request={viewingRequest} requirementName={requirementName} onClose={() => setViewingRequest(undefined)} /> : null}
218: 
219:       {activeSeriesFromParam ? (
220:         <SeriesDetailDialog
221:           series={activeSeriesFromParam}
222:           requests={requests}
223:           requirementName={requirementName}
224:           canWrite={canWrite}
225:           subjectId={subjectId}
226:           onClose={() => navigate(orgPath(`/subjects/${subjectId}/requests`))}
227:           onEdit={() => {
228:             navigate(orgPath(`/subjects/${subjectId}/requests`));
229:             setSeriesDialog({ mode: "edit", series: activeSeriesFromParam });
230:           }}
231:           onCancel={() => {
232:             navigate(orgPath(`/subjects/${subjectId}/requests`));
233:             setCancelingSeries(activeSeriesFromParam);
234:           }}
235:           showToast={showToast}
236:         />
237:       ) : null}
238:     </div>
239:   );
240: }
241: 
242: function SeriesTable({
243:   series,
244:   requirementName,
245:   canWrite,
246:   orgPath,
247:   subjectId,
248:   onEdit,
249:   onCancel,
250:   showToast,
251: }: {
252:   series: DocumentRequestSeries[];
253:   requirementName: (id: string) => string;
254:   canWrite: boolean;
255:   orgPath: (path: string) => string;
256:   subjectId: string;
257:   onEdit: (series: DocumentRequestSeries) => void;
258:   onCancel: (series: DocumentRequestSeries) => void;
259:   showToast: (message: string) => void;
260: }) {
261:   const columns: DataTableColumn<DocumentRequestSeries>[] = [
262:     {
263:       key: "requirement",
264:       header: "Requisito",
265:       primary: true,
266:       // Design deviation, named: the spec's "clique na linha abre o detalhe" assumes whole-row
267:       // activation, which `DataTable` (`components/ui/DataTable.tsx`) does not support today (no
268:       // per-row click/keyboard-activation prop exists) - extending it is out of this block's
269:       // scope (implementation-sequencing-plan.md §2: extend the design system only when a
270:       // journey demonstrates the need, never speculatively). The primary cell's own link is the
271:       // reachable equivalent - same destination (`/series/:seriesId`), same detail dialog.
272:       render: (s) => <Link to={orgPath(`/subjects/${subjectId}/series/${s.seriesId}`)}>{requirementName(s.requirementId)}</Link>,
273:     },
274:     { key: "status", header: "Status", render: (s) => <StatusBadge presentation={presentDocumentRequestSeriesStatus(s.status)} /> },
275:     {
276:       key: "cadence",
277:       header: "Recorrência",
278:       render: (s) => (s.status === "ACTIVE" ? cadenceLabel(s.cadence.intervalDays) : "—"),
279:     },
280:     { key: "nextDueAt", header: "Próxima geração", numeric: true, render: (s) => (s.status === "ACTIVE" ? formatAbsoluteDate(s.nextDueAt) : "—") },
281:     { key: "recipient", header: "Destinatário", render: (s) => (s.recipientEmail ? s.recipientEmail : <span className="u-text-attention">Não definido</span>) },
282:     {
283:       key: "actions",
284:       header: "Ações",
285:       actions: true,
286:       render: (s) =>
287:         !canWrite ? null : s.status === "CANCELLED" ? (
288:           <CellSecondary>Cancelada</CellSecondary>
289:         ) : (
290:           <SeriesRowActions subjectId={subjectId} series={s} onEdit={() => onEdit(s)} onCancel={() => onCancel(s)} showToast={showToast} />
291:         ),
292:     },
293:   ];
294: 
295:   return (
296:     <DataTable
297:       caption="Séries recorrentes"
298:       columns={columns}
299:       rows={series}
300:       rowKey={(s) => s.seriesId}
301:     />
302:   );
303: }
304: 
305: function SeriesRowActions({
306:   subjectId,
307:   series,
308:   onEdit,
309:   onCancel,
310:   showToast,
311: }: {
312:   subjectId: string;
313:   series: DocumentRequestSeries;
314:   onEdit: () => void;
315:   onCancel: () => void;
316:   showToast: (message: string) => void;
317: }) {
318:   const materialize = useMaterializeSeriesAttempt(subjectId, series.seriesId);
319:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | undefined>();
320:   const hasRecipient = Boolean(series.recipientEmail);
321: 
322:   async function handleMaterialize() {
323:     setError(undefined);
324:     try {
325:       await materialize.mutateAsync({ expectedVersion: series.version });
326:       showToast("Solicitação gerada");
327:     } catch (err) {
328:       setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível gerar a solicitação.");
329:     }
330:   }
331: 
332:   return (
333:     <>
334:       <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" pending={materialize.isPending} disabled={!hasRecipient} title={!hasRecipient ? "Defina um destinatário para gerar solicitações desta série" : undefined} onClick={() => void handleMaterialize()}>
335:         {materialize.isPending ? "Gerando…" : "Gerar agora"}
336:       </Button>{" "}
337:       <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onEdit}>
338:         Editar
339:       </Button>{" "}
340:       <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onCancel}>
341:         Cancelar
342:       </Button>
343:       {error ? (
344:         <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert">
345:           {error}
346:         </InlineNotice>
347:       ) : null}
348:     </>
349:   );
350: }
351: 
352: function RequestsTable({ requests, requirementName, onView }: { requests: DocumentRequest[]; requirementName: (id: string) => string; onView: (request: DocumentRequest) => void }) {
353:   const now = new Date();
354:   const columns: DataTableColumn<DocumentRequest>[] = [
355:     { key: "requirement", header: "Requisito", primary: true, render: (r) => requirementName(r.requirementId) },
356:     { key: "createdAt", header: "Gerada em", numeric: true, render: (r) => formatAbsoluteDate(r.createdAt) },
357:     { key: "link", header: "Link do convidado", render: (r) => presentGuestLinkState(r, now) },
358:     {
359:       key: "actions",
360:       header: "Ações",
361:       actions: true,
362:       render: (r) => (
363:         <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onView(r)}>
364:           Ver
365:         </Button>
366:       ),
367:     },
368:   ];
369: 
370:   return <DataTable caption="Solicitações avulsas e materializações" columns={columns} rows={requests} rowKey={(r) => r.documentRequestId} />;
371: }
372: 
373: function CreateAvulsoDialog({ subjectId, requirements, onClose, showToast }: { subjectId: string; requirements: Requirement[]; onClose: () => void; showToast: (message: string) => void }) {
374:   const mutation = useCreateDocumentRequest(subjectId);
375:   const [requirement, setRequirement] = useState<Requirement | null>(null);
376:   const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
377:   // ADR-0016 Decision B: DEFAULT (A22's tenant preference) is the honest default here too - never
378:   // pre-selecting EMAIL, which would silently promise a send this form cannot itself guarantee.
379:   const [deliveryMode, setDeliveryMode] = useState<InitialInviteDeliveryOverride>("DEFAULT");
380:   const [errors, setErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
381: 
382:   async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
383:     event.preventDefault();
384:     if (!requirement) {
385:       setErrors(["Selecione um Requisito."]);
386:       return;
387:     }
388:     // Client-side mirror of the backend's own rule (EMAIL, explicit or resolved via DEFAULT,
389:     // requires a recipientEmail) - only enforceable here for the EXPLICIT case, since DEFAULT's
390:     // real resolution depends on the org's A22 preference, which this form does not read.
391:     if (deliveryMode === "EMAIL" && !email.trim()) {
392:       setErrors(["Informe o e-mail do destinatário para entrega por e-mail automático."]);
393:       return;
394:     }
395:     setErrors([]);
396:     try {
397:       await mutation.mutateAsync({
398:         subjectId,
399:         requirementId: requirement.requirementId,
400:         ...(email.trim() ? { recipientEmail: email.trim() } : {}),
401:         initialInviteDelivery: deliveryMode,
402:       });
403:       showToast("Solicitação criada");
404:       onClose();
405:     } catch (err) {
406:       setErrors([err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível criar a solicitação."]);
407:     }
408:   }
409: 
410:   return (
411:     <Dialog title="Nova solicitação avulsa" onClose={onClose}>
412:       <form onSubmit={(event) => void handleSubmit(event)} noValidate>
413:         <FormErrorSummary errors={errors} />
414:         <Combobox label="Requisito" required options={requirements} value={requirement} onChange={setRequirement} getOptionId={(r) => r.requirementId} getOptionLabel={(r) => r.name} />
415:         <TextField
416:           id="avulso-email"
417:           label="Destinatário"
418:           type="text"
419:           value={email}
420:           onChange={setEmail}
421:           required={deliveryMode === "EMAIL"}
422:           hint="E-mail que receberá o link de convidado, se a entrega for por e-mail."
423:         />
424:         <RadioGroup
425:           legend="Entrega do convite inicial"
426:           variant="cards"
427:           options={DELIVERY_OVERRIDE_OPTIONS}
428:           value={deliveryMode}
429:           onChange={(value) => setDeliveryMode(value as InitialInviteDeliveryOverride)}
430:           required
431:         />
432:         {/* ADR-0016 Decision B: never promises unconditional e-mail - the effective mode follows
433:             the organization's A22 configuration unless explicitly overridden above. */}
434:         <InlineNotice tone="neutral">
435:           O link de convidado sem login é sempre gerado. O envio automático por e-mail (quando aplicável) é confirmado apenas como aceito pelo provedor — não como recebido.
436:         </InlineNotice>
437:         <Button type="submit" variant="primary" pending={mutation.isPending}>
438:           {mutation.isPending ? "Criando…" : "Criar solicitação"}
439:         </Button>{" "}
440:         <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
441:           Cancelar
442:         </Button>
443:       </form>
444:     </Dialog>
445:   );
446: }
447: 
448: function SeriesDialog({
449:   subjectId,
450:   mode,
451:   requirements,
452:   onClose,
453:   showToast,
454: }: {
455:   subjectId: string;
456:   mode: { mode: "create" } | { mode: "edit"; series: DocumentRequestSeries };
457:   requirements: Requirement[];
458:   onClose: () => void;
459:   showToast: (message: string) => void;
460: }) {
461:   const isEdit = mode.mode === "edit";
462:   const editingSeries = isEdit ? mode.series : undefined;
463:   const createMutation = useCreateSeries(subjectId);
464:   const recipientMutation = useUpdateSeriesRecipient(subjectId, editingSeries?.seriesId ?? "");
465:   const queryClient = useQueryClient();
466:   const { organizationId } = useActiveOrganization();
467:   const [requirement, setRequirement] = useState<Requirement | null>(requirements[0] ?? null);
468:   const [email, setEmail] = useState(editingSeries?.recipientEmail ?? "");
469:   const [cadenceOption, setCadenceOption] = useState(NAMED_CADENCES[0]?.value ?? "MONTHLY");
470:   const [advancedDays, setAdvancedDays] = useState("");
471:   const [advanced, setAdvanced] = useState(false);
472:   const [errors, setErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
473:   const [conflict, setConflict] = useState(false);
474: 
475:   const intervalDays = advanced ? Number(advancedDays) || 0 : (NAMED_CADENCES.find((c) => c.value === cadenceOption)?.intervalDays ?? 0);
476:   const previewDate = new Date(Date.now() + intervalDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
477: 
478:   async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
479:     event.preventDefault();
480:     setErrors([]);
481:     setConflict(false);
482: 
483:     if (isEdit && editingSeries) {
484:       try {
485:         await recipientMutation.mutateAsync({ recipientEmail: email.trim() || null, expectedVersion: editingSeries.version });
486:         showToast("Série atualizada");
487:         onClose();
488:       } catch (err) {
489:         if (isConflict(err)) {
490:           setConflict(true);
491:           // The dialog's own `editingSeries` is a snapshot passed in at open time, never a live
492:           // subscription - it cannot repopulate itself in place. Invalidating here at least makes
493:           // sure the LIST behind this dialog shows the current version the moment the user closes
494:           // and reopens it (the copy below tells them to do exactly that, honestly, rather than
495:           // implying an in-place refresh that never happens).
496:           if (organizationId) void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.documentArchive.series(organizationId, subjectId) });
497:           return;
498:         }
499:         setErrors([err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível atualizar a série."]);
500:       }
501:       return;
502:     }
503: 
504:     if (!requirement) {
505:       setErrors(["Selecione um Requisito."]);
506:       return;
507:     }
508:     if (!email.trim()) {
509:       setErrors(["Informe o e-mail do destinatário."]);
510:       return;
511:     }
512:     if (intervalDays <= 0) {
513:       setErrors(["Informe uma recorrência válida."]);
514:       return;
515:     }
516:     try {
517:       await createMutation.mutateAsync({ subjectId, requirementId: requirement.requirementId, cadence: { intervalDays }, recipientEmail: email.trim() });
518:       showToast("Série criada");
519:       onClose();
520:     } catch (err) {
521:       setErrors([err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível criar a série."]);
522:     }
523:   }
524: 
525:   const pending = createMutation.isPending || recipientMutation.isPending;
526: 
527:   return (
528:     <Dialog title={isEdit ? "Editar série" : "Nova série recorrente"} onClose={onClose}>
529:       <form onSubmit={(event) => void handleSubmit(event)} noValidate>
530:         <FormErrorSummary errors={errors} />
531:         {conflict ? (
532:           <InlineNotice tone="warning" announce="alert">
533:             Esta série foi alterada por outra pessoa. Feche este formulário e abra "Editar" novamente para ver os valores atuais antes de salvar.
534:           </InlineNotice>
535:         ) : null}
536:         {isEdit && editingSeries ? (
537:           <p>
538:             <strong>Requisito:</strong> {editingSeries.requirementId}
539:           </p>
540:         ) : (
541:           <Combobox label="Requisito" required options={requirements} value={requirement} onChange={setRequirement} getOptionId={(r) => r.requirementId} getOptionLabel={(r) => r.name} />
542:         )}
543:         <TextField id="series-email" label="Destinatário" value={email} onChange={setEmail} required={!isEdit} hint="E-mail que receberá o link de convidado a cada ciclo." />
544:         {isEdit ? (
545:           <InlineNotice tone="neutral">
546:             A recorrência desta série ({editingSeries ? cadenceLabel(editingSeries.cadence.intervalDays) : ""}) não pode ser alterada aqui — apenas o destinatário. Alterar a
547:             recorrência de uma série existente ainda não é uma capacidade do sistema.
548:           </InlineNotice>
549:         ) : (
550:           <>
551:             {!advanced ? (
552:               <SelectField id="series-cadence" label="Recorrência" value={cadenceOption} onChange={setCadenceOption} required options={NAMED_CADENCES.map((c) => ({ value: c.value, label: c.label }))} />
553:             ) : (
554:               <TextField id="series-cadence-days" label="Recorrência (dias)" value={advancedDays} onChange={setAdvancedDays} required hint="Número de dias entre cada geração." />
555:             )}
556:             <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setAdvanced((v) => !v)}>
557:               {advanced ? "Usar opção nomeada" : "Modo avançado"}
558:             </Button>
559:             {intervalDays > 0 ? <p>Próxima geração estimada: {formatAbsoluteDate(previewDate.toISOString())}</p> : null}
560:             {/* ADR-0016 Decision B: séries não têm override próprio de entrega - apenas o padrão
561:                 de A22 (Configurações > Entrega de solicitação) se aplica a cada materialização. */}
562:             <InlineNotice tone="neutral">
563:               A entrega do convite de cada ciclo segue o padrão configurado em Configurações &gt; Entrega de solicitação, sem opção própria aqui.
564:             </InlineNotice>
565:           </>
566:         )}
567:         <Button type="submit" variant="primary" pending={pending}>
568:           {pending ? "Salvando…" : isEdit ? "Salvar" : "Criar série"}
569:         </Button>{" "}
570:         <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
571:           Cancelar
572:         </Button>
573:       </form>
574:     </Dialog>
575:   );
576: }

645: function SeriesDetailDialog({
646:   series,
647:   requests,
648:   requirementName,
649:   canWrite,
650:   subjectId,
651:   onClose,
652:   onEdit,
653:   onCancel,
654:   showToast,
655: }: {
656:   series: DocumentRequestSeries;
657:   requests: DocumentRequest[];
658:   requirementName: (id: string) => string;
659:   canWrite: boolean;
660:   subjectId: string;
661:   onClose: () => void;
662:   onEdit: () => void;
663:   onCancel: () => void;
664:   showToast: (message: string) => void;
665: }) {
666:   const materialize = useMaterializeSeriesAttempt(subjectId, series.seriesId);
667:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | undefined>();
668:   const cycleRequests = requests.filter((r) => r.seriesId === series.seriesId);
669: 
670:   async function handleMaterialize() {
671:     setError(undefined);
672:     try {
673:       await materialize.mutateAsync({ expectedVersion: series.version });
674:       showToast("Solicitação gerada");
675:     } catch (err) {
676:       setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível gerar a solicitação.");
677:     }
678:   }
679: 
680:   return (
681:     <Dialog title={requirementName(series.requirementId)} onClose={onClose}>
682:       <p>
683:         <strong>Status:</strong> <StatusBadge presentation={presentDocumentRequestSeriesStatus(series.status)} />
684:       </p>
685:       {series.status === "ACTIVE" ? (
686:         <>
687:           <p>
688:             <strong>Recorrência:</strong> {cadenceLabel(series.cadence.intervalDays)}
689:           </p>
690:           <p>
691:             <strong>Próxima geração:</strong> {formatAbsoluteDate(series.nextDueAt)}
692:           </p>
693:         </>
694:       ) : null}
695:       <p>
696:         <strong>Destinatário:</strong> {series.recipientEmail ?? "Não definido"}
697:       </p>
698:       {cycleRequests.length === 0 ? (
699:         <p>Aguardando a primeira geração em {formatAbsoluteDate(series.nextDueAt)}.</p>
700:       ) : (
701:         <p>{cycleRequests.length} solicitação(ões) já geradas por esta série.</p>
702:       )}
703:       {error ? (
704:         <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert">
705:           {error}
706:         </InlineNotice>
707:       ) : null}
708:       {canWrite && series.status === "ACTIVE" ? (
709:         <>
710:           <Button variant="primary" pending={materialize.isPending} disabled={!series.recipientEmail} onClick={() => void handleMaterialize()}>
711:             {materialize.isPending ? "Gerando…" : "Gerar agora"}
712:           </Button>{" "}
713:           <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onEdit}>
714:             Editar
715:           </Button>{" "}
716:           <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onCancel}>
717:             Cancelar série
718:           </Button>{" "}
719:         </>
720:       ) : null}
721:       <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
722:         Fechar
723:       </Button>
724:     </Dialog>
725:   );
726: }
FILE: frontend/src/routes/subjects/DossierExport.tsx
1: /**
2:  * A17 — Exportar dossiê do fornecedor (Block 10, D-2xx). `docarchive:dossier-export`, OWNER+ADMIN
3:  * exclusively - NO assignee/responsible-party exception (D-205, confirmed correct in a 2026-09-10
4:  * audit, deliberately not softened here even for the Subject's own MEMBER/VIEWER owner).
5:  *
6:  * Real 3-stage wizard now buildable in full - the preview/confirm/download routes all exist for
7:  * real (`api/requirements.ts`'s own header comment on `downloadDossierExport` has the full
8:  * investigation, including the real gap this screen works around: there is no dedicated "get run
9:  * status" route, so `pollDossierExportRun` derives status from the download route's own
10:  * success/ConflictError outcome).
11:  *
12:  * `runId` lives in the URL (`?runId=`) rather than only in component state, matching the spec's
13:  * own requirement that leaving this screen mid-generation and returning later resumes the SAME
14:  * run rather than forcing a fresh preview - the only way to resume the real backend run without a
15:  * dedicated "get run" route is to already know its id, and the URL is the one place that survives
16:  * a full reload.
17:  *
18:  * Codex review round (Block 10) findings, fixed:
19:  *  - Polling used `retry: false`, so a single transient network/5xx hiccup on the FIRST poll
20:  *    permanently stopped polling forever (no data ever landed for `refetchInterval`'s own
21:  *    condition to re-arm) - a small bounded retry now absorbs a transient blip, and a genuine
22:  *    persistent failure surfaces its own retry action instead of leaving the screen stuck on
23:  *    "Gerando dossiê…" forever.
24:  *  - A failure at the final "Baixar dossiê" click (expired run, network, any non-2xx) was
25:  *    silently swallowed - now surfaces a real error with its own retry.
26:  *  - `confirmDossierExport`'s CONFLICT can come from two backend causes indistinguishable from
27:  *    this side (`document-archive-service.ts`'s `confirmDossierExport`: a genuine `scopeHash`
28:  *    mismatch, OR a concurrent-write OCC race) - verified directly against that file. This
29:  *    frontend can never actually detect "the Subject's data changed", contrary to what the
30:  *    previous copy claimed ("O escopo mudou...") - the message is now neutral about the cause
31:  *    while still offering the same safe recovery (refresh the preview).
32:  *  - Format selection (PDF/Excel) is now the existing `RadioGroup` component (real native radio
33:  *    inputs, one group, accessible name/state/relationship) instead of two unrelated buttons.
34:  *  - Focus now moves to each new stage's own heading/status on every real stage transition
35:  *    (`preview` -> `generating` -> `ready`/`failed`), per the spec's explicit requirement -
36:  *    previously only `aria-live` announced it, never actual focus.
37:  */
38: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
39: import { Link, useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
40: import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
41: import { Check, Download } from "lucide-react";
42: import { useOrgPath } from "../../routing/useOrgPath.js";
43: import { useActiveOrganization } from "../../auth/ActiveOrganizationContext.js";
44: import { useSubject } from "../../hooks/useSubject.js";
45: import { useCurrentMembershipRole } from "../../hooks/useCurrentMembershipRole.js";
46: import { queryKeys } from "../../api/queryKeys.js";
47: import { previewDossierExport, confirmDossierExport, pollDossierExportRun, downloadDossierExport, type DossierPreviewRow } from "../../api/requirements.js";
48: import { ApiError, isConflict } from "../../api/errors.js";
49: import { InitialLoading, ErrorState, EmptyState, AsyncFeedback } from "../../components/AsyncStates.js";
50: import { PageHeader, Panel } from "../../components/ui/Layout.js";
51: import { Button } from "../../components/ui/Button.js";
52: import { InlineNotice } from "../../components/ui/InlineNotice.js";
53: import { RadioGroup } from "../../components/ui/RadioGroup.js";
54: import type { DossierExportFormat, DossierExportRunStatus, MembershipRole } from "../../api/types.js";
55: 
56: function canExportDossier(role: MembershipRole | undefined): boolean {
57:   return role === "OWNER" || role === "ADMIN";
58: }
59: 
60: const GENERATING_STATUSES = new Set<DossierExportRunStatus>(["CONFIRMED", "GENERATING"]);
61: const SLOW_GENERATION_THRESHOLD_MS = 45_000;
62: const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 3_000;
63: const POLL_RETRY_COUNT = 2;
64: 
65: export function DossierExport() {
66:   const { subjectId = "" } = useParams<{ subjectId: string }>();
67:   const orgPath = useOrgPath();
68:   const { organizationId, switching } = useActiveOrganization();
69:   const role = useCurrentMembershipRole();
70:   const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
71:   const runId = searchParams.get("runId") ?? undefined;
72: 
73:   const subjectQuery = useSubject(subjectId);
74: 
75:   const [preview, setPreview] = useState<
76:     { kind: "idle" } | { kind: "loading" } | { kind: "loaded"; runId: string; scopeHash: string; rows: DossierPreviewRow[] } | { kind: "error"; message: string }
77:   >({ kind: "idle" });
78:   const [format, setFormat] = useState<DossierExportFormat>("pdf");
79:   const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
80:   const [confirmError, setConfirmError] = useState<string | undefined>();
81:   const [staleScope, setStaleScope] = useState(false);
82:   const generationStartedAt = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
83: 
84:   const canExport = canExportDossier(role);
85: 
86:   useEffect(() => {
87:     if (!canExport || runId) return;
88:     let cancelled = false;
89:     setPreview({ kind: "loading" });
90:     setStaleScope(false);
91:     previewDossierExport(subjectId)
92:       .then(({ run, rows }) => {
93:         if (!cancelled) setPreview({ kind: "loaded", runId: run.runId, scopeHash: run.scopeHash, rows });
94:       })
95:       .catch((err: unknown) => {
96:         if (!cancelled) setPreview({ kind: "error", message: err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível pré-visualizar o dossiê." });
97:       });
98:     return () => {
99:       cancelled = true;
100:     };
101:   }, [subjectId, runId, canExport]);
102: 
103:   const pollQuery = useQuery<{ status: DossierExportRunStatus }, unknown>({
104:     // Holistic frontend review fix: this key used to be a bare `["dossier-export-run", ...]`
105:     // array, outside the `["org", organizationId, ...]` prefix org-switch cancellation relies on
106:     // (ActiveOrganizationContext.tsx) - switching organizations mid-poll never cancelled it.
107:     queryKey: queryKeys.documentArchive.dossierRun(organizationId ?? "", subjectId, runId ?? ""),
108:     queryFn: ({ signal }) => pollDossierExportRun(subjectId, runId ?? "", { signal }),
109:     enabled: Boolean(runId) && canExport && Boolean(organizationId) && !switching,
110:     retry: POLL_RETRY_COUNT,
111:     refetchInterval: (query) => (query.state.data && GENERATING_STATUSES.has(query.state.data.status) ? POLL_INTERVAL_MS : false),
112:   });
113: 
114:   useEffect(() => {
115:     if (runId && generationStartedAt.current === undefined) generationStartedAt.current = Date.now();
116:     if (!runId) generationStartedAt.current = undefined;
117:   }, [runId]);
118: 
119:   if (role !== undefined && !canExport) {
120:     return (
121:       <>
122:         <PageHeader title="Exportar dossiê" />
123:         <Panel padded>
124:           <EmptyState kind="permission-limited" message="Exportar dossiê é restrito a OWNER e ADMIN desta organização." />
125:         </Panel>
126:       </>
127:     );
128:   }
129: 
130:   if (subjectQuery.isPending) return <InitialLoading label="Carregando fornecedor…" />;
131:   if (subjectQuery.isError) {
132:     const message = subjectQuery.error instanceof ApiError ? subjectQuery.error.message : "Não foi possível carregar este fornecedor.";
133:     return <ErrorState message={message} onRetry={() => void subjectQuery.refetch()} />;
134:   }
135:   const subject = subjectQuery.data.subject;
136: 
137:   async function handleConfirm() {
138:     if (preview.kind !== "loaded") return;
139:     setConfirming(true);
140:     setConfirmError(undefined);
141:     try {
142:       await confirmDossierExport(subjectId, preview.runId, preview.scopeHash);
143:       setSearchParams({ runId: preview.runId });
144:     } catch (err) {
145:       if (isConflict(err)) {
146:         setStaleScope(true);
147:         return;
148:       }
149:       setConfirmError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível gerar o dossiê agora.");
150:     } finally {
151:       setConfirming(false);
152:     }
153:   }
154: 
155:   function handleRefreshPreview() {
156:     setPreview({ kind: "idle" });
157:     setStaleScope(false);
158:     // Re-runs the effect above on next render (runId is still undefined at this stage).
159:     void previewDossierExport(subjectId)
160:       .then(({ run, rows }) => setPreview({ kind: "loaded", runId: run.runId, scopeHash: run.scopeHash, rows }))
161:       .catch((err: unknown) => setPreview({ kind: "error", message: err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível pré-visualizar o dossiê." }));
162:   }
163: 
164:   function handleNewDossier() {
165:     setSearchParams({});
166:   }
167: 
168:   return (
169:     <>
170:       <PageHeader above={<Link to={orgPath(`/subjects/${subjectId}`)}>← Voltar para {subject.displayName}</Link>} title="Exportar dossiê" description={`${subject.displayName} · pacote de conformidade em PDF ou Excel.`} />
171:       <Panel padded>
172:         {!runId ? (
173:           <PreviewStage
174:             preview={preview}
175:             format={format}
176:             onFormatChange={setFormat}
177:             staleScope={staleScope}
178:             confirming={confirming}
179:             confirmError={confirmError}
180:             onConfirm={() => void handleConfirm()}
181:             onRefreshPreview={handleRefreshPreview}
182:           />
183:         ) : (
184:           <GenerationStage
185:             subjectId={subjectId}
186:             runId={runId}
187:             pollQuery={pollQuery}
188:             format={format}
189:             generationStartedAt={generationStartedAt.current}
190:             onNewDossier={handleNewDossier}
191:           />
192:         )}
193:       </Panel>
194:     </>
195:   );
196: }
197: 
198: function PreviewStage({
199:   preview,
200:   format,
201:   onFormatChange,
202:   staleScope,
203:   confirming,
204:   confirmError,
205:   onConfirm,
206:   onRefreshPreview,
207: }: {
208:   preview: { kind: "idle" } | { kind: "loading" } | { kind: "loaded"; runId: string; scopeHash: string; rows: DossierPreviewRow[] } | { kind: "error"; message: string };
209:   format: DossierExportFormat;
210:   onFormatChange: (format: DossierExportFormat) => void;
211:   staleScope: boolean;
212:   confirming: boolean;
213:   confirmError: string | undefined;
214:   onConfirm: () => void;
215:   onRefreshPreview: () => void;
216: }) {
217:   const headingRef = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
218:   useEffect(() => {
219:     if (preview.kind === "loaded") headingRef.current?.focus();
220:   }, [preview.kind]);
221: 
222:   if (preview.kind === "idle" || preview.kind === "loading") {
223:     return <InitialLoading label="Carregando pré-visualização…" />;
224:   }
225:   if (preview.kind === "error") {
226:     return <ErrorState message={preview.message} onRetry={onRefreshPreview} />;
227:   }
228: 
229:   return (
230:     <div>
231:       <section aria-labelledby="dossier-scope-heading">
232:         <h3 id="dossier-scope-heading" ref={headingRef} tabIndex={-1}>
233:           Escopo (congelado nesta pré-visualização)
234:         </h3>
235:         <p>Requisitos incluídos: {preview.rows.length}</p>
236:         <p title="Identifica exatamente esta versão do escopo. Se os dados mudarem antes de você confirmar, você será avisado e poderá atualizar a pré-visualização.">Hash do escopo: {preview.scopeHash}</p>
237:       </section>
238:       <RadioGroup
239:         legend="Formato"
240:         required
241:         name="dossier-format"
242:         value={format}
243:         onChange={(value) => onFormatChange(value as DossierExportFormat)}
244:         options={[
245:           { value: "pdf", label: "PDF" },
246:           { value: "xlsx", label: "Excel" },
247:         ]}
248:       />
249:       <InlineNotice tone="info">Apenas OWNER e ADMIN podem exportar o dossiê, mesmo que sejam o responsável direto pelo fornecedor.</InlineNotice>
250:       {staleScope ? (
251:         <InlineNotice tone="warning" announce="alert" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={onRefreshPreview}>Atualizar pré-visualização</Button>}>
252:           {/* Codex review round finding: the backend's confirm route returns the SAME generic
253:               CONFLICT for a real scopeHash mismatch AND for an unrelated concurrent-write OCC race
254:               (verified directly against confirmDossierExport in document-archive-service.ts) -
255:               this frontend cannot actually tell which happened, so the copy stays neutral about
256:               cause rather than claiming a specific one it can't prove. */}
257:           Não foi possível confirmar a geração agora. Atualize a pré-visualização e tente novamente.
258:         </InlineNotice>
259:       ) : (
260:         <>
261:           {confirmError ? (
262:             <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert">
263:               {confirmError}
264:             </InlineNotice>
265:           ) : null}
266:           <Button variant="primary" icon={Check} pending={confirming} onClick={onConfirm}>
267:             {confirming ? "Gerando…" : "Confirmar e gerar"}
268:           </Button>
269:         </>
270:       )}
271:     </div>
272:   );
273: }
274: 
275: function GenerationStage({
276:   subjectId,
277:   runId,
278:   pollQuery,
279:   format,
280:   generationStartedAt,
281:   onNewDossier,
282: }: {
283:   subjectId: string;
284:   runId: string;
285:   pollQuery: { data?: { status: DossierExportRunStatus }; isPending: boolean; isError: boolean; error: unknown; refetch: () => void };
286:   format: DossierExportFormat;
287:   generationStartedAt: number | undefined;
288:   onNewDossier: () => void;
289: }) {
290:   const [slow, setSlow] = useState(false);
291:   const [downloadError, setDownloadError] = useState<string | undefined>();
292:   const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState(false);
293:   const statusHeadingRef = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
294:   const status = pollQuery.data?.status;
295:   const stageKey = pollQuery.isPending ? "pending" : pollQuery.isError ? "error" : (status ?? "unknown");
296: 
297:   useEffect(() => {
298:     statusHeadingRef.current?.focus();
299:   }, [stageKey]);
300: 
301:   useEffect(() => {
302:     if (!generationStartedAt) return;
303:     const elapsed = Date.now() - generationStartedAt;
304:     if (elapsed >= SLOW_GENERATION_THRESHOLD_MS) {
305:       setSlow(true);
306:       return;
307:     }
308:     const timeout = setTimeout(() => setSlow(true), SLOW_GENERATION_THRESHOLD_MS - elapsed);
309:     return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
310:   }, [generationStartedAt]);
311: 
312:   async function handleDownload() {
313:     setDownloadError(undefined);
314:     setDownloading(true);
315:     try {
316:       const { downloadUrl } = await downloadDossierExport(subjectId, runId, format);
317:       window.location.assign(downloadUrl);
318:     } catch (err) {
319:       // Codex review round finding: this used to be an empty catch, silently doing nothing on a
320:       // real failure (expired run, network, any non-2xx) - now surfaces it with a retry.
321:       setDownloadError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Não foi possível baixar o dossiê agora.");
322:     } finally {
323:       setDownloading(false);
324:     }
325:   }
326: 
327:   if (pollQuery.isPending) {
328:     return <AsyncFeedback state="PROCESSING" message="Gerando dossiê…" />;
329:   }
330: 
331:   // Codex review round finding: `retry: false` used to mean a single transient poll failure
332:   // stopped polling forever (no data ever existed for `refetchInterval`'s own re-arm condition) -
333:   // the query now retries a couple of times on its own; if it still fails, offer a real retry
334:   // instead of leaving the screen stuck on a generic "Gerando…" with no way forward.
335:   if (pollQuery.isError) {
336:     return (
337:       <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => void pollQuery.refetch()}>Tentar novamente</Button>}>
338:         Não foi possível verificar o andamento da geração agora.
339:       </InlineNotice>
340:     );
341:   }
342: 
343:   if (status === "READY") {
344:     return (
345:       <div>
346:         <h3 ref={statusHeadingRef} tabIndex={-1} className="u-visually-hidden">
347:           Dossiê gerado
348:         </h3>
349:         <InlineNotice tone="success" announce="status">
350:           Dossiê gerado.
351:         </InlineNotice>
352:         <p>Formato selecionado: {format === "pdf" ? "PDF" : "Excel"}.</p>
353:         {downloadError ? (
354:           <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert">
355:             {downloadError}
356:           </InlineNotice>
357:         ) : null}
358:         <Button variant="primary" icon={Download} pending={downloading} onClick={() => void handleDownload()}>
359:           {downloading ? "Baixando…" : "Baixar dossiê"}
360:         </Button>
361:       </div>
362:     );
363:   }
364: 
365:   if (status === "FAILED" || status === "TOO_LARGE") {
366:     return (
367:       <div>
368:         <h3 ref={statusHeadingRef} tabIndex={-1} className="u-visually-hidden">
369:           {status === "TOO_LARGE" ? "Pacote muito grande" : "Falha na geração"}
370:         </h3>
371:         <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={onNewDossier}>Tentar novamente</Button>}>
372:           {status === "TOO_LARGE" ? "O pacote excedeu o limite de tamanho." : "Não foi possível gerar o dossiê."}
373:         </InlineNotice>
374:       </div>
375:     );
376:   }
377: 
378:   return (
379:     <div>
380:       <h3 ref={statusHeadingRef} tabIndex={-1} className="u-visually-hidden">
381:         Gerando dossiê
382:       </h3>
383:       <AsyncFeedback state="PROCESSING" message="Gerando dossiê…" />
384:       {slow ? (
385:         <InlineNotice tone="neutral">Isso está demorando mais que o esperado. O processamento continua no backend — você pode voltar mais tarde.</InlineNotice>
